                : 'border-transparent text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-200'
            }`}
          >
            Timeline & Notes
            <span className="px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded-full text-[10px] bg-zinc-800 text-zinc-300">
              {application.timeline.length}
            </span>
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('snapshot')}
            className={`pb-2.5 font-semibold transition border-b-2 flex items-center gap-1.5 ${
              activeTab === 'snapshot'
                ? 'border-indigo-500 text-white'
                : 'border-transparent text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-200'
            }`}
          >
            <FileText className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-indigo-400" />
            Linked CV Snapshot
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Modal Body */}
        <div className="p-6 overflow-y-auto space-y-4 text-xs">
          {/* TAB 1: DETAILS */}
          {activeTab === 'details' && (
            <div className="space-y-4">
              {/* Pipeline Status Selector */}
              <div>
                <label className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-zinc-400 uppercase tracking-wider block mb-1.5">
                  Current Pipeline Stage
                </label>
                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-2">
                  {PIPELINE_STAGES.map((stg) => {
                    const isSelected = status === stg.id;
                    return (
                      <button
                        key={stg.id}
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => setStatus(stg.id)}
                        className={`p-2.5 rounded-xl border text-left font-medium transition ${
                          isSelected
                            ? `${stg.bgColor} ${stg.borderColor} ${stg.color} ring-1 ring-current`
                            : 'bg-zinc-950/60 border-zinc-800/80 text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-200'
                        }`}
                      >
                        <div className="font-bold">{stg.label}</div>
                      </button>
                    );
                  })}
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Compensation & Work Info */}
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3 pt-2">
                <div>
                  <label className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-zinc-400 block mb-1">
                    Salary / Compensation Range
                  </label>
                  <div className="relative">
                    <DollarSign className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-zinc-500 absolute left-3 top-2.5" />
                    <input
                      type="text"
                      value={salaryRange}
                      onChange={(e) => setSalaryRange(e.target.value)}
                      placeholder="e.g. $95,000 - $110,000 USD"
                      className="w-full pl-8 pr-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-zinc-950 border border-zinc-800 text-zinc-200 focus:outline-none focus:border-indigo-500 text-xs"
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-zinc-400 block mb-1">
                    Location & Work Policy
                  </label>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-zinc-950 border border-zinc-800 text-zinc-300">
                    <MapPin className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-zinc-500" />
                    <span>{application.location}</span>
                    <span className="ml-auto px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800 capitalize">
                      {application.workType}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Recruiter Contacts */}
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                <div>
                  <label className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-zinc-400 block mb-1">
                    Recruiter / Hiring Manager Name
                  </label>
                  <div className="relative">
                    <User className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-zinc-500 absolute left-3 top-2.5" />
                    <input
                      type="text"
                      value={recruiterName}
                      onChange={(e) => setRecruiterName(e.target.value)}
                      placeholder="e.g. Sarah Jenkins"
                      className="w-full pl-8 pr-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-zinc-950 border border-zinc-800 text-zinc-200 focus:outline-none focus:border-indigo-500 text-xs"
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-zinc-400 block mb-1">
                    Recruiter Email Address
                  </label>
                  <div className="relative">
                    <Mail className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-zinc-500 absolute left-3 top-2.5" />
                    <input
                      type="email"
                      value={recruiterEmail}
                      onChange={(e) => setRecruiterEmail(e.target.value)}
                      placeholder="e.g. sarah@company.com"
                      className="w-full pl-8 pr-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-zinc-950 border border-zinc-800 text-zinc-200 focus:outline-none focus:border-indigo-500 text-xs"
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Notes & Prep Questions */}
              <div>
                <label className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-zinc-400 block mb-1">
                  Interview Notes & Target Preparation
                </label>
                <textarea
                  rows={4}
                  value={interviewNotes}
                  onChange={(e) => setInterviewNotes(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Record questions asked, team structure, tech stack hints, or salary negotiation notes..."
                  className="w-full p-3 rounded-xl bg-zinc-950 border border-zinc-800 text-zinc-200 focus:outline-none focus:border-indigo-500 leading-relaxed font-sans text-xs"
                />
              </div>

              {/* External Link */}
              {application.jobUrl && (
                <div className="pt-1">
                  <a
                    href={application.jobUrl}
                    target="_blank"
                    rel="noreferrer"
                    className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-indigo-400 hover:text-indigo-300 text-xs font-semibold"
                  >
                    <ExternalLink className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                    Open Original Job Posting ({application.jobUrl})
                  </a>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          )}

          {/* TAB 2: TIMELINE */}
          {activeTab === 'timeline' && (
            <div className="space-y-4">
              {/* Event Add Input */}
              <div className="flex gap-2">
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={newTimelineNote}
                  onChange={(e) => setNewTimelineNote(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Log an update (e.g. 'Completed 2nd tech interview with Engineering Lead')..."
                  className="flex-1 px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-zinc-950 border border-zinc-800 text-zinc-200 text-xs focus:outline-none focus:border-indigo-500"
                />
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    if (newTimelineNote.trim()) {
                      const updatedTimeline = [
                        ...application.timeline,
                        {
                          id: `tl_add_${Date.now()}`,
                          status,
                          date: new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0],
                          note: newTimelineNote.trim(),
                        },
                      ];
                      onUpdate({
                        ...application,
                        timeline: updatedTimeline,
                      });
                      setNewTimelineNote('');
                    }
                  }}
                  className="px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-500 text-white font-semibold text-xs transition"
                >
                  <Plus className="h-4 w-4" />
                </button>
              </div>

              {/* Chronological List */}
              <div className="space-y-3 relative before:absolute before:left-3.5 before:top-2 before:bottom-2 before:w-0.5 before:bg-zinc-800">
                {application.timeline.map((evt, idx) => {
                  const stageMatch = PIPELINE_STAGES.find((s) => s.id === evt.status);
                  return (
                    <div key={evt.id || idx} className="flex items-start gap-3 relative pl-1">
                      <div className="h-6 w-6 rounded-full bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-700 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 z-10 text-[10px]">
                        •
                      </div>
                      <div className="flex-1 p-3 rounded-xl bg-zinc-950 border border-zinc-800/80 space-y-1">
                        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                          <span className={`font-semibold ${stageMatch?.color || 'text-zinc-300'}`}>
                            {stageMatch?.label || evt.status}
                          </span>
                          <span className="text-[10px] text-zinc-500 font-mono">
                            {evt.date}
                          </span>
                        </div>
                        <p className="text-zinc-300 leading-relaxed text-xs">
                          {evt.note}
                        </p>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* TAB 3: LINKED CV SNAPSHOT */}
          {activeTab === 'snapshot' && (
            <div className="space-y-4">
              <div className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-zinc-950 border border-zinc-800 flex items-center justify-between">
                <div>
                  <h4 className="font-bold text-zinc-100">
                    Exact Tailored CV Snapshot Linked to this Application
                  </h4>
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-zinc-400 mt-0.5">
                    Immutable snapshot of the resume version submitted for {application.company}.
                  </p>
                </div>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={handleDownloadSubmittedPdf}
                  disabled={isDownloadingPdf}
                  className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 text-white transition disabled:opacity-50"
                >
                  {isDownloadingPdf ? (
                    <Loader2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5 animate-spin" />
                  ) : (
                    <Download className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                  )}
                  Download PDF
                </button>
              </div>

              {/* Snapshot Summary Preview */}
              <div className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-zinc-950 border border-zinc-800/80 space-y-3 font-mono text-xs">
                <div>
                  <span className="text-zinc-500 block text-[10px] uppercase font-bold">
                    Submitted Headline:
                  </span>
                  <span className="text-zinc-200">
                    {application.tailoredCvSnapshot.personalInfo.headline}
                  </span>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <span className="text-zinc-500 block text-[10px] uppercase font-bold">
                    Submitted Summary:
                  </span>
                  <p className="text-zinc-300 font-sans text-xs leading-relaxed">
                    {application.tailoredCvSnapshot.personalInfo.summary}
                  </p>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <span className="text-zinc-500 block text-[10px] uppercase font-bold">
                    Top Skills Highlighted:
                  </span>
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1 mt-1 font-sans">
                    {[
                      ...application.tailoredCvSnapshot.skills.languages,
                      ...application.tailoredCvSnapshot.skills.frameworks,
                      ...application.tailoredCvSnapshot.skills.databases,
                    ]
                      .slice(0, 10)
                      .map((s, idx) => (
                        <span
                          key={idx}
                          className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[11px] bg-zinc-900 text-zinc-300 border border-zinc-800"
                        >
                          {s}
                        </span>
                      ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Modal Footer */}
        <div className="px-6 py-4 border-t border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-950 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => {
              if (confirm(`Are you sure you want to remove ${application.company} from your pipeline?`)) {
                onDelete(application.id);
                onClose();
              }
            }}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs text-rose-400 hover:text-rose-300 hover:bg-rose-500/10 rounded-lg transition"
          >
            <Trash2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
            Delete
          </button>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onClose}
              className="px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold rounded-xl bg-zinc-800 hover:bg-zinc-700 text-zinc-300 transition"
            >
              Cancel
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={handleSave}
              className="px-5 py-2 text-xs font-semibold rounded-xl bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-500 text-white transition shadow-sm"
            >
              Save Changes
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};
</file>

<file path="src/components/pipeline/KanbanBoard.tsx">
'use client';

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { JobApplicationRecord, ApplicationStatus } from '@/types/pipeline';
import { PIPELINE_STAGES } from '@/data/samplePipeline';
import {
  Building,
  Briefcase,
  MapPin,
  Calendar,
  DollarSign,
  Download,
  ArrowRight,
  MoreVertical,
  CheckCircle2,
  Clock,
  Sparkles,
  ChevronRight,
  ExternalLink,
} from 'lucide-react';

interface KanbanBoardProps {
  applications: JobApplicationRecord[];
  onStatusChange: (applicationId: string, newStatus: ApplicationStatus) => void;
  onSelectApplication: (application: JobApplicationRecord) => void;
  onDownloadPdf: (application: JobApplicationRecord) => void;
}

export const KanbanBoard: React.FC<KanbanBoardProps> = ({
  applications,
  onStatusChange,
  onSelectApplication,
  onDownloadPdf,
}) => {
  const [draggedAppId, setDraggedAppId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [dragOverCol, setDragOverCol] = useState<ApplicationStatus | null>(null);

  // Drag Handlers
  const handleDragStart = (e: React.DragEvent, id: string) => {
    e.dataTransfer.setData('text/plain', id);
    setDraggedAppId(id);
  };

  const handleDragOver = (e: React.DragEvent, colId: ApplicationStatus) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (dragOverCol !== colId) {
      setDragOverCol(colId);
    }
  };

  const handleDragLeave = (e: React.DragEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
  };

  const handleDrop = (e: React.DragEvent, targetColId: ApplicationStatus) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setDragOverCol(null);
    const appId = e.dataTransfer.getData('text/plain') || draggedAppId;
    if (appId) {
      onStatusChange(appId, targetColId);
    }
    setDraggedAppId(null);
  };

  // Get next stage in progression
  const getNextStage = (curr: ApplicationStatus): ApplicationStatus | null => {
    const order: ApplicationStatus[] = ['wishlist', 'applied', 'screening', 'technical', 'offer'];
    const idx = order.indexOf(curr);
    if (idx !== -1 && idx < order.length - 1) {
      return order[idx + 1];
    }
    return null;
  };

  return (
    <div className="overflow-x-auto pb-6">
      <div className="flex gap-4 min-w-[1250px] items-start">
        {PIPELINE_STAGES.map((stage) => {
          const stageApps = applications.filter((app) => app.status === stage.id);
          const isOver = dragOverCol === stage.id;

          return (
            <div
              key={stage.id}
              onDragOver={(e) => handleDragOver(e, stage.id)}
              onDragLeave={handleDragLeave}
              onDrop={(e) => handleDrop(e, stage.id)}
              className={`flex-1 min-w-[280px] max-w-[340px] rounded-2xl border transition flex flex-col max-h-[82vh] ${
                isOver
                  ? 'bg-zinc-900 border-indigo-500/80 ring-2 ring-indigo-500/30'
                  : `${stage.bgColor} ${stage.borderColor}`
              }`}
            >
              {/* Column Header */}
              <div className="p-3.5 border-b border-zinc-800/80 flex items-center justify-between">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <span className={`text-xs font-bold ${stage.color}`}>
                    {stage.label}
                  </span>
                  <span className="px-2 py-0.2 rounded-full text-[10px] font-mono font-bold bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800 text-zinc-400">
                    {stageApps.length}
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Column Cards Container */}
              <div className="p-3 space-y-3 overflow-y-auto flex-1">
                {stageApps.length === 0 ? (
                  <div className="py-8 text-center text-zinc-600 text-xs italic">
                    Drop applications here
                  </div>
                ) : (
                  stageApps.map((app) => {
                    const nextStage = getNextStage(app.status);
                    return (
                      <div
                        key={app.id}
                        draggable
                        onDragStart={(e) => handleDragStart(e, app.id)}
                        onClick={() => onSelectApplication(app)}
                        className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-zinc-950 border border-zinc-800/90 hover:border-zinc-700 shadow-md transition cursor-pointer space-y-2.5 active:scale-[0.99] group"
                      >
                        {/* Company & Match Badge */}
                        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                          <h4 className="text-xs font-bold text-zinc-100 group-hover:text-indigo-300 transition">
                            {app.company}
                          </h4>
                          <span className="px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-bold bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-400 border border-emerald-500/20 shrink-0">
                            {app.matchScore}% Match
                          </span>
                        </div>

                        {/* Job Title */}
                        <p className="text-[11px] font-medium text-zinc-300 line-clamp-1">
                          {app.jobTitle}
                        </p>

                        {/* Location & Compensation */}
                        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5 text-[10px] text-zinc-400">
                          <span className="flex items-center gap-1 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800 capitalize">
                            <MapPin className="h-3 w-3 text-zinc-500" />
                            {app.workType}
                          </span>
                          {app.salaryRange && (
                            <span className="px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800 text-zinc-300 font-mono">
                              {app.salaryRange}
                            </span>
                          )}
                        </div>

                        {/* Bottom Action Footer */}
                        <div className="pt-2 border-t border-zinc-800/80 flex items-center justify-between text-[11px]">
                          <span className="text-[10px] text-zinc-500 font-mono">
                            {app.appliedDate || app.createdAt.split('T')[0]}
                          </span>

                          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5" onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
                            <button
                              type="button"
                              onClick={() => onDownloadPdf(app)}
                              title="Download linked tailored PDF"
                              className="p-1 rounded-md text-zinc-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-zinc-800 transition"
                            >
                              <Download className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                            </button>

                            {nextStage && (
                              <button
                                type="button"
                                onClick={() => onStatusChange(app.id, nextStage)}
                                title={`Advance to ${nextStage}`}
                                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md text-[10px] font-bold bg-zinc-800 hover:bg-indigo-600 text-zinc-300 hover:text-white transition"
                              >
                                Advance
                                <ArrowRight className="h-3 w-3" />
                              </button>
                            )}
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    );
                  })
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};
</file>

<file path="src/components/pipeline/PipelineAnalyticsDashboard.tsx">
'use client';

import React, { useMemo } from 'react';
import { JobApplicationRecord } from '@/types/pipeline';
import { PIPELINE_STAGES } from '@/data/samplePipeline';
import {
  TrendingUp,
  Target,
  Award,
  Clock,
  Briefcase,
  Users,
  CheckCircle2,
  PieChart,
  BarChart3,
  Sparkles,
} from 'lucide-react';

interface PipelineAnalyticsDashboardProps {
  applications: JobApplicationRecord[];
}

export const PipelineAnalyticsDashboard: React.FC<PipelineAnalyticsDashboardProps> = ({
  applications,
}) => {
  const metrics = useMemo(() => {
    const total = applications.length;
    const applied = applications.filter((a) => a.status === 'applied').length;
    const screening = applications.filter((a) => a.status === 'screening').length;
    const technical = applications.filter((a) => a.status === 'technical').length;
    const offer = applications.filter((a) => a.status === 'offer').length;
    const archived = applications.filter((a) => a.status === 'archived').length;
    const wishlist = applications.filter((a) => a.status === 'wishlist').length;

    // Total that moved past applied into interview rounds or offer
    const advanced = screening + technical + offer;
    const submittedTotal = applied + screening + technical + offer + archived;
    const interviewRate =
      submittedTotal > 0 ? Math.round((advanced / submittedTotal) * 100) : 0;

    const offerRate = submittedTotal > 0 ? Math.round((offer / submittedTotal) * 100) : 0;

    const avgMatch =
      total > 0
        ? Math.round(
            applications.reduce((acc, curr) => acc + (curr.matchScore || 0), 0) / total
          )
        : 0;

    // Extract top skills across all applications
    const skillCounts: Record<string, number> = {};
    applications.forEach((app) => {
      const skills = [
        ...app.tailoredCvSnapshot.skills.languages,
        ...app.tailoredCvSnapshot.skills.frameworks,
        ...app.tailoredCvSnapshot.skills.databases,
        ...app.tailoredCvSnapshot.skills.devopsAndCloud,
      ];
      skills.forEach((s) => {
        skillCounts[s] = (skillCounts[s] || 0) + 1;
      });
    });

    const topSkills = Object.entries(skillCounts)
      .sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])
      .slice(0, 8);

    return {
      total,
      wishlist,
      applied,
      screening,
      technical,
      offer,
      archived,
      advanced,
      interviewRate,
      offerRate,
      avgMatch,
      topSkills,
    };
  }, [applications]);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* 4 Top KPI Cards */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
        {/* Total Tracked */}
        <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800 shadow-md space-y-1.5">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs text-zinc-400">
            <span>Total Tracked</span>
            <Briefcase className="h-4 w-4 text-purple-400" />
          </div>
          <div className="text-2xl font-black text-white">{metrics.total}</div>
          <p className="text-[11px] text-zinc-500">
            {metrics.wishlist} in draft wishlist, {metrics.total - metrics.wishlist} active/submitted
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Interview Rate */}
        <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800 shadow-md space-y-1.5">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs text-zinc-400">
            <span>Interview Rate</span>
            <TrendingUp className="h-4 w-4 text-emerald-400" />
          </div>
          <div className="text-2xl font-black text-emerald-400">
            {metrics.interviewRate}%
          </div>
          <p className="text-[11px] text-zinc-500">
            {metrics.advanced} advanced to screening & tech
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Average Match */}
        <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800 shadow-md space-y-1.5">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs text-zinc-400">
            <span>Avg Match Score</span>
            <Target className="h-4 w-4 text-indigo-400" />
          </div>
          <div className="text-2xl font-black text-indigo-300">
            {metrics.avgMatch}%
          </div>
          <p className="text-[11px] text-zinc-500">
            Across tailored ATS resumes
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Offers Extended */}
        <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800 shadow-md space-y-1.5">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs text-zinc-400">
            <span>Job Offers</span>
            <Award className="h-4 w-4 text-amber-400" />
          </div>
          <div className="text-2xl font-black text-amber-300">
            {metrics.offer}
          </div>
          <p className="text-[11px] text-zinc-500">
            {metrics.offerRate}% conversion from applied
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Stage Progression Funnel */}
      <div className="p-5 rounded-2xl bg-zinc-900/90 border border-zinc-800 space-y-4 shadow-md">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
          <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-zinc-200 flex items-center gap-2">
            <BarChart3 className="h-4 w-4 text-indigo-400" />
            Application Funnel & Pipeline Breakdown
          </h4>
          <span className="text-xs text-zinc-400">Real-time status tracking</span>
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-6 gap-3">
          {PIPELINE_STAGES.map((stg) => {
            const count = applications.filter((a) => a.status === stg.id).length;
            const pct = metrics.total > 0 ? Math.round((count / metrics.total) * 100) : 0;
            return (
              <div
                key={stg.id}
                className="p-3.5 rounded-xl bg-zinc-950 border border-zinc-800/80 space-y-1.5"
              >
                <div className="flex justify-between items-center text-xs">
                  <span className={`font-semibold ${stg.color}`}>{stg.label}</span>
                  <span className="font-mono text-zinc-400 font-bold">{count}</span>
                </div>
                <div className="w-full bg-zinc-800 h-1.5 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
                  <div
                    className="bg-indigo-500 h-full rounded-full transition-all duration-500"
                    style={{ width: `${pct}%` }}
                  />
                </div>
                <div className="text-[10px] text-zinc-500">{pct}% of total</div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Top Target Skills Cloud */}
      <div className="p-5 rounded-2xl bg-zinc-900/90 border border-zinc-800 space-y-3 shadow-md">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
          <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-zinc-200 flex items-center gap-2">
            <Sparkles className="h-4 w-4 text-purple-400" />
            Top Skills In-Demand Across Saved Applications
          </h4>
          <span className="text-xs text-zinc-400">Position requirements overlap</span>
        </div>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          {metrics.topSkills.map(([skill, count]) => (
            <div
              key={skill}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-zinc-950 border border-zinc-800 text-xs"
            >
              <span className="text-zinc-200 font-medium">{skill}</span>
              <span className="px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold bg-indigo-500/20 text-indigo-300">
                {count} {count === 1 ? 'job' : 'jobs'}
              </span>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};
</file>

<file path="src/components/pipeline/PipelineView.tsx">
'use client';

import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { JobApplicationRecord, ApplicationStatus } from '@/types/pipeline';
import { getSampleJobApplications, PIPELINE_STAGES } from '@/data/samplePipeline';
import { MasterCvProfile } from '@/types/masterCv';
import { KanbanBoard } from './KanbanBoard';
import { PipelineAnalyticsDashboard } from './PipelineAnalyticsDashboard';
import { ApplicationDetailModal } from './ApplicationDetailModal';
import { AddApplicationModal } from './AddApplicationModal';
import {
  Briefcase,
  LayoutGrid,
  BarChart3,
  List,
  Plus,
  Download,
  Upload,
  RotateCcw,
  CheckCircle2,
  Search,
  ExternalLink,
  MapPin,
  Calendar,
} from 'lucide-react';

interface PipelineViewProps {
  masterProfile: MasterCvProfile;
}

export const PipelineView: React.FC<PipelineViewProps> = ({ masterProfile }) => {
  const [applications, setApplications] = useState<JobApplicationRecord[]>([]);
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<'kanban' | 'analytics' | 'list'>('kanban');
  const [selectedApp, setSelectedApp] = useState<JobApplicationRecord | null>(null);
  const [isAddModalOpen, setIsAddModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [filterStage, setFilterStage] = useState<string>('all');
  const fileInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  // Initialize from localStorage or seed with sample applications
  useEffect(() => {
    try {
      const stored = localStorage.getItem('huntflow_applications');
      if (stored) {
        const parsed = JSON.parse(stored);
        if (Array.isArray(parsed) && parsed.length > 0) {
          setApplications(parsed);
          return;
        }
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.error('Error loading stored applications:', e);
    }
    // Default seed
    const defaultApps = getSampleJobApplications();
    setApplications(defaultApps);
    try {
      localStorage.setItem('huntflow_applications', JSON.stringify(defaultApps));
    } catch {}
  }, []);

  // Sync to localStorage
  const saveApplications = (updated: JobApplicationRecord[]) => {
    setApplications(updated);
    try {
      localStorage.setItem('huntflow_applications', JSON.stringify(updated));
    } catch (e) {
      console.error('Error saving applications to localStorage:', e);
    }
  };

  // Status transition handler
  const handleStatusChange = (appId: string, newStatus: ApplicationStatus) => {
    const stageMatch = PIPELINE_STAGES.find((s) => s.id === newStatus);
    const updated = applications.map((app) => {
      if (app.id === appId && app.status !== newStatus) {
        const newTimeline = [
          ...app.timeline,
          {
            id: `tl_${Date.now()}`,
            status: newStatus,
            date: new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0],
            note: `Advanced to ${stageMatch?.label || newStatus}.`,
          },
        ];
        return {
          ...app,
          status: newStatus,
          timeline: newTimeline,
          updatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
        };
      }
      return app;
    });
    saveApplications(updated);
  };

  // Add application
  const handleAddApplication = (newRecord: JobApplicationRecord) => {
    const updated = [newRecord, ...applications];
    saveApplications(updated);
  };

  // Update application from modal
  const handleUpdateApplication = (updatedApp: JobApplicationRecord) => {
    const updated = applications.map((a) => (a.id === updatedApp.id ? updatedApp : a));
    saveApplications(updated);
    setSelectedApp(null);
  };

  // Delete application
  const handleDeleteApplication = (id: string) => {
    const updated = applications.filter((a) => a.id !== id);
    saveApplications(updated);
    setSelectedApp(null);
  };

  // Reset to default sample
  const handleResetToDemo = () => {
    if (confirm('Reset application pipeline to the initial Abdullah Ademi demo records?')) {
      const defaultApps = getSampleJobApplications();
      saveApplications(defaultApps);
    }
  };

  // Export JSON Backup
  const handleExportBackup = () => {
    const dataStr = 'data:text/json;charset=utf-8,' + encodeURIComponent(JSON.stringify(applications, null, 2));
    const a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = dataStr;
    a.download = `huntflow_applications_backup_${new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]}.json`;
    a.click();
  };

  // Import JSON Backup
  const handleImportBackup = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = (event) => {
      try {
        const parsed = JSON.parse(event.target?.result as string);
        if (Array.isArray(parsed)) {
          saveApplications(parsed);
          alert(`Successfully imported ${parsed.length} job applications!`);
        } else {
          alert('Invalid backup file format.');
        }
      } catch (err) {
        alert('Failed to parse JSON file.');
      }
    };
    reader.readAsText(file);
    e.target.value = '';
  };

  // Download Tailored PDF for an application
  const handleDownloadAppPdf = async (app: JobApplicationRecord) => {
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/cv/download-pdf', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ profile: app.tailoredCvSnapshot }),
      });

      if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Failed to generate PDF');

      const blob = await res.blob();
      const url = window.URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      const a = document.createElement('a');
      a.href = url;
      const safeName = (app.tailoredCvSnapshot.personalInfo.fullName || 'Candidate').replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g, '_');
      const safeCompany = app.company.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g, '_');
      a.download = `${safeName}_${safeCompany}_Resume.pdf`;
      document.body.appendChild(a);
      a.click();
      window.URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
      document.body.removeChild(a);
    } catch (e: any) {
      alert('Could not download PDF: ' + e.message);
    }
  };

  // Filtered applications
  const filteredApps = applications.filter((app) => {
    const matchesSearch =
      app.company.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) ||
      app.jobTitle.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase()) ||
      app.location.toLowerCase().includes(searchQuery.toLowerCase());
    const matchesStage = filterStage === 'all' || app.status === filterStage;
    return matchesSearch && matchesStage;
  });

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* Hidden File Input for Import */}
      <input
        type="file"
        ref={fileInputRef}
        onChange={handleImportBackup}
        accept=".json"
        className="hidden"
      />

      {/* Top Banner Bar */}
      <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-4 p-5 rounded-2xl bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800 shadow-lg">
        <div>
          <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-zinc-100 flex items-center gap-2.5">
            <Briefcase className="h-6 w-6 text-indigo-400" />
            Job Application Pipeline & Versioned CV Tracker
          </h2>
          <p className="text-xs text-zinc-400 mt-1">
            Track interview rounds, manage immutable tailored CV snapshots, and monitor career conversion metrics.
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Action Controls */}
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setIsAddModalOpen(true)}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-500 text-white shadow-md shadow-indigo-900/30 transition"
          >
            <Plus className="h-4 w-4" />
            New Application
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleExportBackup}
            title="Export all applications & snapshots as JSON"
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold bg-zinc-800 hover:bg-zinc-700 text-zinc-200 border border-zinc-700 transition"
          >
            <Download className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
            Export JSON
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()}
            title="Import backup JSON"
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold bg-zinc-800 hover:bg-zinc-700 text-zinc-200 border border-zinc-700 transition"
          >
            <Upload className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
            Import
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleResetToDemo}
            title="Reset to default Abdullah Ademi demo pipeline"
            className="p-2 rounded-xl text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-200 bg-zinc-800/60 border border-zinc-700/60 transition"
          >
            <RotateCcw className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Navigation Sub-Tabs & Search Controls */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3 p-3 rounded-xl bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800">
        <div className="flex items-center bg-zinc-950 p-1 rounded-lg border border-zinc-800 text-xs">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('kanban')}
            className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-md font-medium transition ${
              activeTab === 'kanban'
                ? 'bg-zinc-800 text-zinc-100 shadow-sm'
                : 'text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-200'
            }`}
          >
            <LayoutGrid className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-indigo-400" />
            Kanban Board
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('analytics')}
            className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-md font-medium transition ${
              activeTab === 'analytics'
                ? 'bg-zinc-800 text-zinc-100 shadow-sm'
                : 'text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-200'
            }`}
          >
            <BarChart3 className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-emerald-400" />
            Analytics & Funnel
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setActiveTab('list')}
            className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-md font-medium transition ${
              activeTab === 'list'
                ? 'bg-zinc-800 text-zinc-100 shadow-sm'
                : 'text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-200'
            }`}
          >
            <List className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-purple-400" />
            Table View
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Search & Filter */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <div className="relative">
            <Search className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-zinc-500 absolute left-2.5 top-2" />
            <input
              type="text"
              value={searchQuery}
              onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Search company or title..."
              className="pl-8 pr-3 py-1 rounded-lg bg-zinc-950 border border-zinc-800 text-xs text-zinc-200 focus:outline-none focus:border-indigo-500 w-44 sm:w-56"
            />
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* VIEW 1: KANBAN BOARD */}
      {activeTab === 'kanban' && (
        <KanbanBoard
          applications={filteredApps}
          onStatusChange={handleStatusChange}
          onSelectApplication={(app) => setSelectedApp(app)}
          onDownloadPdf={handleDownloadAppPdf}
        />
      )}

      {/* VIEW 2: ANALYTICS DASHBOARD */}
      {activeTab === 'analytics' && (
        <PipelineAnalyticsDashboard applications={applications} />
      )}

      {/* VIEW 3: TABLE LIST VIEW */}
      {activeTab === 'list' && (
        <div className="p-4 rounded-2xl bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800 overflow-x-auto shadow-md">
          <table className="w-full text-left text-xs">
            <thead className="border-b border-zinc-800 text-zinc-400 font-semibold uppercase text-[10px]">
              <tr>
                <th className="pb-3 pl-2">Company</th>
                <th className="pb-3">Role Title</th>
                <th className="pb-3">Stage</th>
                <th className="pb-3">Match</th>
                <th className="pb-3">Compensation</th>
                <th className="pb-3">Applied Date</th>
                <th className="pb-3 text-right pr-2">Actions</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody className="divide-y divide-zinc-800/80">
              {filteredApps.map((app) => {
                const stageMatch = PIPELINE_STAGES.find((s) => s.id === app.status);
                return (
                  <tr
                    key={app.id}
                    onClick={() => setSelectedApp(app)}
                    className="hover:bg-zinc-800/40 transition cursor-pointer"
                  >
                    <td className="py-3 pl-2 font-bold text-zinc-100">
                      {app.company}
                    </td>
                    <td className="py-3 text-zinc-300 font-medium">
                      {app.jobTitle}
                    </td>
                    <td className="py-3">
                      <span className={`inline-flex px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold border ${stageMatch?.badgeColor || ''}`}>
                        {stageMatch?.label || app.status}
                      </span>
                    </td>
                    <td className="py-3 font-mono text-emerald-400 font-semibold">
                      {app.matchScore}%
                    </td>
                    <td className="py-3 text-zinc-400 font-mono">
                      {app.salaryRange || '—'}
                    </td>
                    <td className="py-3 text-zinc-500 font-mono">
                      {app.appliedDate || app.createdAt.split('T')[0]}
                    </td>
                    <td className="py-3 text-right pr-2" onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => handleDownloadAppPdf(app)}
                        className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-1 rounded bg-zinc-800 hover:bg-zinc-700 text-zinc-300 transition text-[11px]"
                      >
                        <Download className="h-3 w-3" />
                        PDF
                      </button>
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                );
              })}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* DETAIL MODAL */}
      {selectedApp && (
        <ApplicationDetailModal
          application={selectedApp}
          isOpen={Boolean(selectedApp)}
          onClose={() => setSelectedApp(null)}
          onUpdate={handleUpdateApplication}
          onDelete={handleDeleteApplication}
        />
      )}

      {/* ADD APPLICATION MODAL */}
      {isAddModalOpen && (
        <AddApplicationModal
          isOpen={isAddModalOpen}
          onClose={() => setIsAddModalOpen(false)}
          onAdd={handleAddApplication}
          masterProfile={masterProfile}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
};
</file>

<file path="src/components/studio/EducationEditor.tsx">
'use client';

import React from 'react';
import { GraduationCap, Award, Plus, Trash2, Calendar, BookOpen } from 'lucide-react';
import { EducationItem, CertificationItem } from '@/types/masterCv';

interface EducationEditorProps {
  education: EducationItem[];
  certifications: CertificationItem[];
  onChangeEducation: (updated: EducationItem[]) => void;
  onChangeCertifications: (updated: CertificationItem[]) => void;
}

export const EducationEditor: React.FC<EducationEditorProps> = ({
  education,
  certifications,
  onChangeEducation,
  onChangeCertifications,
}) => {
  const handleUpdateEdu = (id: string, updates: Partial<EducationItem>) => {
    onChangeEducation(
      education.map((item) => (item.id === id ? { ...item, ...updates } : item))
    );
  };

  const handleAddEdu = () => {
    const newItem: EducationItem = {
      id: `edu_${Date.now()}`,
      institution: 'University Name',
      degree: "Bachelor's Degree",
      field: 'Computer Science',
      startDate: '2020',
      endDate: '2023',
      gpa: '',
      achievements: [],
    };
    onChangeEducation([...education, newItem]);
  };

  const handleRemoveEdu = (id: string) => {
    onChangeEducation(education.filter((item) => item.id !== id));
  };

  const handleUpdateCert = (id: string, updates: Partial<CertificationItem>) => {
    onChangeCertifications(
      certifications.map((item) => (item.id === id ? { ...item, ...updates } : item))
    );
  };

  const handleAddCert = () => {
    const newCert: CertificationItem = {
      id: `cert_${Date.now()}`,
      name: 'Professional Certificate Name',
      issuer: 'Issuing Organization',
      date: '2024',
    };
    onChangeCertifications([...certifications, newCert]);
  };

  const handleRemoveCert = (id: string) => {
    onChangeCertifications(certifications.filter((item) => item.id !== id));
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* Education Card */}
      <div className="bg-zinc-900/70 border border-zinc-800 rounded-2xl p-6 shadow-xl space-y-5">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between pb-3 border-b border-zinc-800">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="p-2 rounded-xl bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-400 border border-emerald-500/20">
              <GraduationCap className="h-5 w-5" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h3 className="font-semibold text-white text-base">Education & Academic Background</h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-zinc-400">
                Degrees, universities, dates, and relevant software coursework
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleAddEdu}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 text-white transition shadow-sm"
          >
            <Plus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
            Add Degree
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-4">
          {education.map((item) => (
            <div
              key={item.id}
              className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-zinc-950/70 border border-zinc-800 space-y-3"
            >
              <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3 flex-1">
                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-[11px] font-medium text-zinc-400 mb-1">
                      Degree / Qualification
                    </label>
                    <input
                      type="text"
                      value={item.degree}
                      onChange={(e) =>
                        handleUpdateEdu(item.id, { degree: e.target.value })
                      }
                      className="w-full px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800 rounded-lg text-white focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-emerald-500"
                    />
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-[11px] font-medium text-zinc-400 mb-1">
                      Institution / University
                    </label>
                    <input
                      type="text"
                      value={item.institution}
                      onChange={(e) =>
                        handleUpdateEdu(item.id, { institution: e.target.value })
                      }
                      className="w-full px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800 rounded-lg text-white focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-emerald-500"
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>

                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => handleRemoveEdu(item.id)}
                  className="p-1.5 rounded-lg text-zinc-500 hover:text-rose-400 transition"
                >
                  <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4" />
                </button>
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-[11px] font-medium text-zinc-400 mb-1">
                    Major / Field of Study
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    value={item.field || ''}
                    onChange={(e) =>
                      handleUpdateEdu(item.id, { field: e.target.value })
                    }
                    placeholder="e.g. Computer Science"
                    className="w-full px-3 py-1.5 text-xs bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800 rounded-lg text-zinc-200 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-emerald-500"
                  />
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="block text-[11px] font-medium text-zinc-400 mb-1">
                    Dates Attended
                  </label>
                  <div className="flex gap-2">
                    <input
                      type="text"
                      value={item.startDate}
                      onChange={(e) =>
                        handleUpdateEdu(item.id, { startDate: e.target.value })
                      }
                      placeholder="Start (2020)"
                      className="w-1/2 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800 rounded-lg text-zinc-200"
                    />
                    <input
                      type="text"
                      value={item.endDate}
                      onChange={(e) =>
                        handleUpdateEdu(item.id, { endDate: e.target.value })
                      }
                      placeholder="End (2023)"
                      className="w-1/2 px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800 rounded-lg text-zinc-200"
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="block text-[11px] font-medium text-zinc-400 mb-1">
                    GPA / Honors
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    value={item.gpa || ''}
                    onChange={(e) =>
                      handleUpdateEdu(item.id, { gpa: e.target.value })
                    }
                    placeholder="e.g. 3.9 / 4.0 or Honors"
                    className="w-full px-3 py-1.5 text-xs bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800 rounded-lg text-zinc-200 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-emerald-500"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Certifications Card */}
      <div className="bg-zinc-900/70 border border-zinc-800 rounded-2xl p-6 shadow-xl space-y-5">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between pb-3 border-b border-zinc-800">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <div className="p-2 rounded-xl bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-400 border border-amber-500/20">
              <Award className="h-5 w-5" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h3 className="font-semibold text-white text-base">Certifications & Licenses</h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-zinc-400">
                Industry certifications, verified badges, and licenses
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleAddCert}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold bg-amber-600 hover:bg-amber-500 text-white transition shadow-sm"
          >
            <Plus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
            Add Certification
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-3">
          {certifications.map((cert) => (
            <div
              key={cert.id}
              className="p-3.5 rounded-xl bg-zinc-950/70 border border-zinc-800 flex items-center justify-between gap-3"
            >
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3 flex-1">
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={cert.name}
                  onChange={(e) => handleUpdateCert(cert.id, { name: e.target.value })}
                  placeholder="Certification Name"
                  className="px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800 rounded-lg text-white"
                />
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={cert.issuer}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    handleUpdateCert(cert.id, { issuer: e.target.value })
                  }
                  placeholder="Issuer (e.g. AWS, Meta)"
                  className="px-3 py-1.5 text-xs bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800 rounded-lg text-zinc-200"
                />
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={cert.date}
                  onChange={(e) => handleUpdateCert(cert.id, { date: e.target.value })}
                  placeholder="Year / Date"
                  className="px-3 py-1.5 text-xs bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800 rounded-lg text-zinc-200"
                />
              </div>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => handleRemoveCert(cert.id)}
                className="p-1 rounded text-zinc-500 hover:text-rose-400 transition"
              >
                <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4" />
              </button>
            </div>
          ))}
          {certifications.length === 0 && (
            <div className="text-center py-6 text-xs text-zinc-500">
              No certifications added yet. Click &quot;Add Certification&quot; above.
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};
</file>

import React from 'react';

              onChange={(e) => handleChange('headline', e.target.value)}
