<file path="src/app/api/ingest/link/route.ts">
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { ExternalLink } from '@/types/ingestion';

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  let rawUrl = '';
  try {
    const body = await req.json();
    rawUrl = typeof body?.url === 'string' ? body.url : '';
    const url = rawUrl;

    if (!url || typeof url !== 'string') {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Valid URL is required.' }, { status: 400 });
    }

    let parsedUrl: URL;
    try {
      parsedUrl = new URL(url.startsWith('http') ? url : `https://${url}`);
    } catch {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Invalid URL format provided.' }, { status: 400 });
    }

    const controller = new AbortController();
    const timeout = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 8000);

    const res = await fetch(parsedUrl.toString(), {
      signal: controller.signal,
      headers: {
        'User-Agent': 'Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36 (KHTML, like Gecko) Chrome/120.0.0.0 Safari/537.36 HuntFlow/1.0',
        Accept: 'text/html,application/xhtml+xml,application/xml;q=0.9,*/*;q=0.8',
      },
    });

    clearTimeout(timeout);

    if (!res.ok) {
      return NextResponse.json(
        { error: `Website returned status ${res.status}: ${res.statusText}` },
        { status: 400 }
      );
    }

    const html = await res.text();

    // Extract title
    const titleMatch = html.match(/<title[^>]*>([^<]+)<\/title>/i);
    const title = titleMatch ? titleMatch[1].trim() : parsedUrl.hostname;

    // Extract meta description
    const metaDescMatch =
      html.match(/<meta\s+name=["']description["']\s+content=["']([^"']+)["']/i) ||
      html.match(/<meta\s+property=["']og:description["']\s+content=["']([^"']+)["']/i);
    const metaDescription = metaDescMatch ? metaDescMatch[1].trim() : '';

    // Strip scripts, styles, svg, and tags to obtain textual content
    const cleanBody = html
      .replace(/<script\b[^<]*(?:(?!<\/script>)<[^<]*)*<\/script>/gi, ' ')
      .replace(/<style\b[^<]*(?:(?!<\/style>)<[^<]*)*<\/style>/gi, ' ')
      .replace(/<svg\b[^<]*(?:(?!<\/svg>)<[^<]*)*<\/svg>/gi, ' ')
      .replace(/<nav\b[^<]*(?:(?!<\/nav>)<[^<]*)*<\/nav>/gi, ' ')
      .replace(/<footer\b[^<]*(?:(?!<\/footer>)<[^<]*)*<\/footer>/gi, ' ')
      .replace(/<[^>]+>/g, ' ')
      .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
      .trim();

    // Extract snippet
    const snippet = metaDescription
      ? `${metaDescription} — ${cleanBody.slice(0, 500)}...`
      : cleanBody.slice(0, 600);

    const linkItem: ExternalLink = {
      id: `link_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 6)}`,
      url: parsedUrl.toString(),
      title,
      snippet,
      status: 'success',
      fetchedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    };

    return NextResponse.json({ link: linkItem });
  } catch (error) {
    console.warn('Link scraping warning:', error);
    // Return a graceful fallback link so the user's workflow is never interrupted
    const urlStr = rawUrl;
    let host = 'External Link';
    try {
      host = new URL(urlStr.startsWith('http') ? urlStr : `https://${urlStr}`).hostname;
    } catch {
      // ignore
    }

    const fallbackLink: ExternalLink = {
      id: `link_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 6)}`,
      url: urlStr,
      title: host,
      snippet: `External reference provided by candidate (${host}). Web crawler could not reach server.`,
      status: 'success',
      fetchedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    };

    return NextResponse.json({
      link: fallbackLink,
      warning: error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Could not fetch external page',
    });
  }
}
</file>

<file path="src/app/api/ingest/sample/route.ts">
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import fs from 'fs';
import path from 'path';
import { detectSections } from '@/lib/corpusAggregator';
import { ParsedDocument } from '@/types/ingestion';
import { getDefaultMasterProfile } from '@/lib/deterministicSynthesizer';

export async function GET() {
  try {
    const defaultProfile = getDefaultMasterProfile();
    const candidatePdfPath = path.join(process.cwd(), 'Cv info', 'ABDULLAH_ADEMI(1).pdf');
    let size = 5200;

    if (fs.existsSync(candidatePdfPath)) {
      size = fs.statSync(candidatePdfPath).size;
    }

    const upToDateSampleCorpus = `
ABDULLAH ADEMI
${defaultProfile.personalInfo.headline}
${defaultProfile.personalInfo.location} • ${defaultProfile.personalInfo.phone} • ${defaultProfile.personalInfo.email} • ${defaultProfile.personalInfo.github} • ${defaultProfile.personalInfo.website} • ${defaultProfile.personalInfo.linkedin}

PROFESSIONAL SUMMARY
${defaultProfile.personalInfo.summary}

EDUCATION
${defaultProfile.education[0]?.institution}    ${defaultProfile.education[0]?.startDate} – ${defaultProfile.education[0]?.endDate}
${defaultProfile.education[0]?.degree} in ${defaultProfile.education[0]?.field} — GPA: ${defaultProfile.education[0]?.gpa}

EXPERIENCE
${defaultProfile.experience[0]?.role}    ${defaultProfile.experience[0]?.startDate} – ${defaultProfile.experience[0]?.endDate}
${defaultProfile.experience[0]?.company}    ${defaultProfile.experience[0]?.location}
${defaultProfile.experience[0]?.bullets.map((b) => '• ' + b).join('\n')}

PROJECTS & TECHNICAL INITIATIVES
${defaultProfile.projects.map((p) => `${p.title} | ${p.techStack.join(', ')}    ${p.startDate} – ${p.endDate}\nImpact: ${p.impactMetric}\n${p.bullets.map((b) => '• ' + b).join('\n')}`).join('\n\n')}

TECHNICAL SKILLS
Languages: ${defaultProfile.skills.languages.join(', ')}
Frameworks & Libraries: ${defaultProfile.skills.frameworks.join(', ')}
Databases: ${defaultProfile.skills.databases.join(', ')}
DevOps & Cloud: ${defaultProfile.skills.devopsAndCloud.join(', ')}
Architecture & Concepts: ${defaultProfile.skills.toolsAndConcepts.join(', ')}
`.trim();

    const wordCount = upToDateSampleCorpus.split(/\s+/).length;
    const charCount = upToDateSampleCorpus.length;
    const sectionsDetected = detectSections(upToDateSampleCorpus);

    const doc: ParsedDocument = {
      id: `sample_doc_abdullah_${Date.now()}`,
      name: 'ABDULLAH_ADEMI.pdf',
      size,
      type: 'pdf',
      extractedText: upToDateSampleCorpus,
      pageCount: 1,
      charCount,
      wordCount,
      uploadedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      sectionsDetected,
    };

    return NextResponse.json({
      document: doc,
      profile: defaultProfile,
      recommendedGithubUsername: 'abdullah1053',
      recommendedBio: defaultProfile.personalInfo.summary,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Error loading sample CV:', error);
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Failed to load sample CV' },
      { status: 500 }
    );
  }
}
</file>

<file path="src/components/studio/ExperienceEditor.tsx">
'use client';

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import {
  Briefcase,
  Plus,
  Trash2,
  Sparkles,
  CheckCircle2,
  Calendar,
  MapPin,
  Building,
  Check,
  ChevronDown,
  ChevronUp,
  Eye,
  EyeOff,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { WorkExperienceItem } from '@/types/masterCv';

interface ExperienceEditorProps {
  experience: WorkExperienceItem[];
  onChange: (updated: WorkExperienceItem[]) => void;
  onOptimizeBullet: (bullet: string, context?: string) => Promise<string>;
}

export const ExperienceEditor: React.FC<ExperienceEditorProps> = ({
  experience,
  onChange,
  onOptimizeBullet,
}) => {
  const [optimizingIndex, setOptimizingIndex] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [newBulletInputs, setNewBulletInputs] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [collapsedItems, setCollapsedItems] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});
  const [collapsedBullets, setCollapsedBullets] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});

  const toggleCollapseItem = (id: string) => {
    setCollapsedItems((prev) => ({ ...prev, [id]: !prev[id] }));
  };

  const toggleCollapseBullets = (id: string) => {
    setCollapsedBullets((prev) => ({ ...prev, [id]: !prev[id] }));
  };

  const handleUpdateItem = (id: string, updates: Partial<WorkExperienceItem>) => {
    onChange(
      experience.map((item) => (item.id === id ? { ...item, ...updates } : item))
    );
  };

  const handleToggleHideItem = (id: string) => {
    const item = experience.find((i) => i.id === id);
    if (!item) return;
    handleUpdateItem(id, { isHidden: !item.isHidden });
  };

  const handleToggleHideBullet = (itemId: string, bulletIndex: number) => {
    const item = experience.find((i) => i.id === itemId);
    if (!item) return;
    const currentHidden = new Set(item.hiddenBullets || []);
    if (currentHidden.has(bulletIndex)) {
      currentHidden.delete(bulletIndex);
    } else {
      currentHidden.add(bulletIndex);
    }
    handleUpdateItem(itemId, { hiddenBullets: Array.from(currentHidden) });
  };

  const handleToggleHideAllBullets = (itemId: string) => {
    const item = experience.find((i) => i.id === itemId);
    if (!item) return;
    const allHidden = (item.hiddenBullets || []).length === item.bullets.length;
    handleUpdateItem(itemId, {
      hiddenBullets: allHidden ? [] : item.bullets.map((_, idx) => idx),
    });
  };

  const handleAddItem = () => {
    const newItem: WorkExperienceItem = {
      id: `exp_${Date.now()}`,
      company: 'New Company',
      role: 'Software Engineer',
      location: 'City, Country',
      startDate: '2023',
      endDate: 'Present',
      isCurrent: true,
      isHidden: false,
      hiddenBullets: [],
      bullets: [
        'Engineered high-performance web systems by designing modular microservices.',
      ],
    };
    onChange([newItem, ...experience]);
  };

  const handleRemoveItem = (id: string) => {
    onChange(experience.filter((item) => item.id !== id));
  };

  const handleUpdateBullet = (itemId: string, bulletIndex: number, text: string) => {
    const item = experience.find((i) => i.id === itemId);
    if (!item) return;

    const newBullets = [...item.bullets];
    newBullets[bulletIndex] = text;
    handleUpdateItem(itemId, { bullets: newBullets });
  };

  const handleRemoveBullet = (itemId: string, bulletIndex: number) => {
    const item = experience.find((i) => i.id === itemId);
    if (!item) return;

    const newBullets = item.bullets.filter((_, idx) => idx !== bulletIndex);
    const newHidden = (item.hiddenBullets || [])
      .filter((idx) => idx !== bulletIndex)
      .map((idx) => (idx > bulletIndex ? idx - 1 : idx));
    handleUpdateItem(itemId, { bullets: newBullets, hiddenBullets: newHidden });
  };

  const handleAddBullet = (itemId: string) => {
    const text = (newBulletInputs[itemId] || '').trim();
    if (!text) return;

    const item = experience.find((i) => i.id === itemId);
    if (!item) return;

    handleUpdateItem(itemId, { bullets: [...item.bullets, text] });
    setNewBulletInputs((prev) => ({ ...prev, [itemId]: '' }));
  };

  const handleAiOptimizeBullet = async (itemId: string, bulletIndex: number) => {
    const item = experience.find((i) => i.id === itemId);
    if (!item) return;

    const bullet = item.bullets[bulletIndex];
    if (!bullet) return;

    const optKey = `${itemId}_${bulletIndex}`;
    setOptimizingIndex(optKey);
    try {
      const optimized = await onOptimizeBullet(bullet, `${item.role} at ${item.company}`);
      handleUpdateBullet(itemId, bulletIndex, optimized);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Failed to optimize bullet:', err);
    } finally {
      setOptimizingIndex(null);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="bg-zinc-900/70 border border-zinc-800 rounded-2xl p-6 shadow-xl space-y-6">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between pb-3 border-b border-zinc-800">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="p-2 rounded-xl bg-blue-500/10 text-blue-400 border border-blue-500/20">
            <Briefcase className="h-5 w-5" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <h3 className="font-semibold text-white text-base">Work Experience & XYZ Bullets</h3>
            <p className="text-xs text-zinc-400">
              Each bullet follows Google’s XYZ formula: Accomplished [X], as measured by [Y], by doing [Z]
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={handleAddItem}
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-500 text-white transition shadow-sm"
        >
          <Plus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
          Add Role
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-6">
        {experience.map((item) => {
          const isCollapsed = Boolean(collapsedItems[item.id]);
          const isBulletsCollapsed = Boolean(collapsedBullets[item.id]);
          const hiddenBulletsSet = new Set(item.hiddenBullets || []);
          const visibleBulletsCount = item.bullets.length - hiddenBulletsSet.size;

          return (
            <div
              key={item.id}
              className={`p-5 rounded-2xl border transition space-y-4 ${
                item.isHidden
                  ? 'bg-zinc-950/40 border-dashed border-amber-500/30 opacity-75'
                  : 'bg-zinc-950/70 border-zinc-800 hover:border-zinc-700/80'
              }`}
            >
              {/* Top row: Role, Company, Collapse toggle & Actions */}
              <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3 pb-3 border-b border-zinc-800/80">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-1">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => toggleCollapseItem(item.id)}
                    className="p-1 rounded-lg text-zinc-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-zinc-800 transition shrink-0"
                    title={isCollapsed ? 'Expand role details' : 'Collapse role details'}
                  >
                    {isCollapsed ? <ChevronDown className="h-4 w-4" /> : <ChevronUp className="h-4 w-4" />}
                  </button>

                  {isCollapsed ? (
                    <div
                      className="flex-1 cursor-pointer flex items-center justify-between gap-3 flex-wrap"
                      onClick={() => toggleCollapseItem(item.id)}
                    >
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
                        <span className="font-semibold text-sm text-white">
                          {item.role || 'Untitled Role'}
                        </span>
                        <span className="text-xs text-zinc-400">
                          at <span className="text-zinc-200">{item.company || 'Company'}</span>
                        </span>
                        <span className="text-[11px] text-zinc-500 font-mono">
                          • {item.startDate || 'Start'} - {item.endDate || 'Present'}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        <span className="text-[10px] px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-zinc-800 text-zinc-300 font-mono">
                          {item.bullets.length} bullets ({visibleBulletsCount} on CV)
                        </span>
                        {item.isHidden && (
                          <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-semibold bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-400 border border-amber-500/20 flex items-center gap-1">
                            <EyeOff className="h-3 w-3" /> Hidden from CV
                          </span>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  ) : (
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3 flex-1">
                      <div>
                        <label className="block text-[11px] font-medium text-zinc-400 mb-1">
                          Job Title / Role
                        </label>
                        <input
                          type="text"
                          value={item.role}
                          onChange={(e) => handleUpdateItem(item.id, { role: e.target.value })}
                          className="w-full px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800 rounded-lg text-white focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500"
                        />
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <label className="block text-[11px] font-medium text-zinc-400 mb-1">
                          Company Name
                        </label>
                        <input
                          type="text"
                          value={item.company}
                          onChange={(e) => handleUpdateItem(item.id, { company: e.target.value })}
                          className="w-full px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800 rounded-lg text-white focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500"
                        />
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>

                {/* Right Action buttons */}
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 self-end sm:self-center shrink-0">
                  {item.isHidden && !isCollapsed && (
                    <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-semibold bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-400 border border-amber-500/20 flex items-center gap-1">
                      <EyeOff className="h-3 w-3" /> Hidden from CV
                    </span>
                  )}
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => handleToggleHideItem(item.id)}
                    className={`p-1.5 rounded-lg border transition ${
                      item.isHidden
                        ? 'bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-400 border-amber-500/30 hover:bg-amber-500/20'
                        : 'bg-zinc-900 text-zinc-400 border-zinc-800 hover:text-white hover:border-zinc-700'
                    }`}
                    title={item.isHidden ? 'Click to include this role in CV' : 'Click to hide this role from CV'}
                  >
                    {item.isHidden ? <EyeOff className="h-4 w-4" /> : <Eye className="h-4 w-4" />}
                  </button>

                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => handleRemoveItem(item.id)}
                    className="p-1.5 rounded-lg text-zinc-500 hover:text-rose-400 hover:bg-zinc-900 transition"
                    title="Remove experience"
                  >
                    <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4" />
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Collapsed body is hidden, expanded body shows details */}
              {!isCollapsed && (
                <>
                  {/* Middle row: Location, Dates, Current Status */}
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3">
                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-[11px] font-medium text-zinc-400 mb-1">
                        Location
                      </label>
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        value={item.location}
                        onChange={(e) =>
                          handleUpdateItem(item.id, { location: e.target.value })
                        }
                        className="w-full px-3 py-1.5 text-xs bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800 rounded-lg text-zinc-200 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500"
                      />
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-[11px] font-medium text-zinc-400 mb-1">
                        Start Date
                      </label>
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        value={item.startDate}
                        onChange={(e) =>
                          handleUpdateItem(item.id, { startDate: e.target.value })
                        }
                        placeholder="e.g. Mar 2023"
                        className="w-full px-3 py-1.5 text-xs bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800 rounded-lg text-zinc-200 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500"
                      />
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-[11px] font-medium text-zinc-400 mb-1">
                        End Date
                      </label>
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        value={item.endDate}
                        onChange={(e) =>
                          handleUpdateItem(item.id, { endDate: e.target.value })
                        }
                        placeholder="e.g. Present"
                        className="w-full px-3 py-1.5 text-xs bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800 rounded-lg text-zinc-200 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500"
                      />
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* Bullets List */}
                  <div className="space-y-3 pt-2">
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between flex-wrap gap-2">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        <label className="block text-[11px] font-semibold text-zinc-300 uppercase tracking-wider">
                          XYZ Impact Bullets ({item.bullets.length})
                        </label>
                        <span className="text-[10px] text-zinc-400 font-mono">
                          • {visibleBulletsCount} visible on CV
                          {hiddenBulletsSet.size > 0 && (
                            <span className="text-amber-400 ml-1">({hiddenBulletsSet.size} hidden)</span>
                          )}
                        </span>
                      </div>

                      <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                        {item.bullets.length > 0 && (
                          <>
                            <button
                              type="button"
                              onClick={() => handleToggleHideAllBullets(item.id)}
                              className="text-[10px] px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-zinc-900 hover:bg-zinc-800 text-zinc-300 border border-zinc-800 transition"
                            >
                              {hiddenBulletsSet.size === item.bullets.length ? 'Unhide All' : 'Hide All Bullets'}
                            </button>
                            <button
                              type="button"
                              onClick={() => toggleCollapseBullets(item.id)}
                              className="text-[10px] px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-zinc-900 hover:bg-zinc-800 text-zinc-300 border border-zinc-800 transition flex items-center gap-1"
                            >
                              {isBulletsCollapsed ? <ChevronDown className="h-3 w-3" /> : <ChevronUp className="h-3 w-3" />}
                              {isBulletsCollapsed ? 'Expand Bullets' : 'Collapse Bullets'}
                            </button>
                          </>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    {!isBulletsCollapsed && (
                      <div className="space-y-2.5">
                        {item.bullets.map((bullet, bIdx) => {
                          const isOptimizing = optimizingIndex === `${item.id}_${bIdx}`;
                          const isBulletHidden = hiddenBulletsSet.has(bIdx);

                          return (
                            <div
                              key={bIdx}
                              className={`p-3 rounded-xl border transition space-y-2 ${
                                isBulletHidden
                                  ? 'bg-zinc-950/40 border-dashed border-amber-500/20 opacity-75'
                                  : 'bg-zinc-900/80 border-zinc-800/80 hover:border-zinc-700/80'
                              }`}
                            >
                              <div className="flex items-start gap-2">
                                <span className={`text-sm mt-1 ${isBulletHidden ? 'text-amber-500/60' : 'text-zinc-500'}`}>
                                  {isBulletHidden ? <EyeOff className="h-3.5 w-3.5 mt-0.5" /> : '•'}
                                </span>
                                <textarea
                                  rows={2}
                                  value={bullet}
                                  onChange={(e) =>
                                    handleUpdateBullet(item.id, bIdx, e.target.value)
                                  }
                                  className={`flex-1 p-2 text-xs rounded-lg placeholder-zinc-500 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500 leading-relaxed resize-none border ${
                                    isBulletHidden
                                      ? 'bg-zinc-950/60 border-dashed border-zinc-800 text-zinc-400'
                                      : 'bg-zinc-950 border-zinc-800 text-zinc-100'
                                  }`}
                                />
                                <button
                                  type="button"
                                  onClick={() => handleToggleHideBullet(item.id, bIdx)}
                                  className={`p-1.5 rounded-lg border transition ${
                                    isBulletHidden
                                      ? 'bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-400 border-amber-500/30 hover:bg-amber-500/20'
                                      : 'text-zinc-400 hover:text-white border-transparent hover:bg-zinc-800'
                                  }`}
                                  title={isBulletHidden ? 'Include this bullet on CV' : 'Hide this bullet from CV'}
                                >
                                  {isBulletHidden ? <EyeOff className="h-3.5 w-3.5" /> : <Eye className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />}
                                </button>
                                <button
                                  type="button"
                                  onClick={() => handleRemoveBullet(item.id, bIdx)}
                                  className="p-1.5 rounded-lg text-zinc-500 hover:text-rose-400 hover:bg-zinc-800 transition"
                                  title="Remove bullet"
                                >
                                  <Trash2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                                </button>
                              </div>

                              <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[11px] pt-1">
                                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                                  <span className="text-zinc-500">
                                    Formula: <span className="text-blue-400">Accomplished [X]</span>,{' '}
                                    <span className="text-purple-400">as measured by [Y]</span>,{' '}
                                    <span className="text-emerald-400">by doing [Z]</span>
                                  </span>
                                  {isBulletHidden && (
                                    <span className="px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded text-[9px] font-semibold bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-400 border border-amber-500/20">
                                      Hidden from CV & PDF
                                    </span>
                                  )}
                                </div>

                                <button
                                  type="button"
                                  disabled={isOptimizing}
                                  onClick={() => handleAiOptimizeBullet(item.id, bIdx)}
                                  className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-[11px] font-medium bg-indigo-500/10 hover:bg-indigo-500/20 text-indigo-300 border border-indigo-500/30 transition disabled:opacity-50"
                                >
                                  <Sparkles className="h-3 w-3 text-amber-400" />
                                  {isOptimizing ? 'Optimizing...' : '⚡ AI Optimize (XYZ)'}
                                </button>
                              </div>
                            </div>
                          );
                        })}
                      </div>
                    )}

                    {/* Add Bullet Input */}
                    <div className="flex gap-2 pt-1">
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        value={newBulletInputs[item.id] || ''}
                        onChange={(e) =>
                          setNewBulletInputs((prev) => ({
                            ...prev,
                            [item.id]: e.target.value,
                          }))
                        }
                        onKeyDown={(e) => {
                          if (e.key === 'Enter') {
                            e.preventDefault();
                            handleAddBullet(item.id);
                          }
                        }}
                        placeholder="Add a new experience achievement bullet..."
                        className="flex-1 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800 rounded-lg text-white placeholder-zinc-500 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-500"
                      />
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => handleAddBullet(item.id)}
                        className="px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium rounded-lg bg-zinc-800 hover:bg-zinc-700 text-zinc-200 border border-zinc-700 transition flex items-center gap-1"
                      >
                        <Plus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                        Add Bullet
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </>
              )}
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};
</file>

<file path="src/components/studio/ProjectsEditor.tsx">
'use client';

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import {
  FolderGit2,
  Plus,
  Trash2,
  Sparkles,
  ExternalLink,
  Code2,
  X,
  TrendingUp,
  Eye,
  EyeOff,
  ChevronDown,
  ChevronUp,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { GithubIcon } from '@/components/icons/GithubIcon';
import { ProjectItem } from '@/types/masterCv';

interface ProjectsEditorProps {
  projects: ProjectItem[];
  onChange: (updated: ProjectItem[]) => void;
  onOptimizeBullet: (bullet: string, context?: string) => Promise<string>;
}

export const ProjectsEditor: React.FC<ProjectsEditorProps> = ({
  projects,
  onChange,
  onOptimizeBullet,
}) => {
  const [optimizingKey, setOptimizingKey] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [techInputs, setTechInputs] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [newBulletInputs, setNewBulletInputs] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [collapsedProjects, setCollapsedProjects] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});
  const [collapsedBullets, setCollapsedBullets] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});

  const toggleCollapseProject = (id: string) => {
    setCollapsedProjects((prev) => ({ ...prev, [id]: !prev[id] }));
  };

  const toggleCollapseBullets = (id: string) => {
    setCollapsedBullets((prev) => ({ ...prev, [id]: !prev[id] }));
  };

  const handleUpdateProject = (id: string, updates: Partial<ProjectItem>) => {
    onChange(
      projects.map((item) => (item.id === id ? { ...item, ...updates } : item))
    );
  };

  const handleToggleHideProject = (id: string) => {
    const proj = projects.find((p) => p.id === id);
    if (!proj) return;
    handleUpdateProject(id, { isHidden: !proj.isHidden });
  };

  const handleToggleHideBullet = (projId: string, bulletIndex: number) => {
    const proj = projects.find((p) => p.id === projId);
    if (!proj) return;
    const currentHidden = new Set(proj.hiddenBullets || []);
    if (currentHidden.has(bulletIndex)) {
      currentHidden.delete(bulletIndex);
    } else {
      currentHidden.add(bulletIndex);
    }
    handleUpdateProject(projId, { hiddenBullets: Array.from(currentHidden) });
  };

  const handleToggleHideAllBullets = (projId: string) => {
    const proj = projects.find((p) => p.id === projId);
    if (!proj) return;
    const allHidden = (proj.hiddenBullets || []).length === proj.bullets.length;
    handleUpdateProject(projId, {
      hiddenBullets: allHidden ? [] : proj.bullets.map((_, idx) => idx),
    });
  };

  const handleAddProject = () => {
    const newProj: ProjectItem = {
      id: `proj_${Date.now()}`,
      title: 'New High-Impact Project',
      role: 'Full-Stack Developer',
      techStack: ['TypeScript', 'Next.js', 'PostgreSQL'],
      impactMetric: 'Scaled system with measurable throughput gains',
      isHidden: false,
      hiddenBullets: [],
      bullets: [
        'Architected modern web application implementing scalable RESTful services and responsive UI.',
      ],
    };
    onChange([newProj, ...projects]);
  };

  const handleRemoveProject = (id: string) => {
    onChange(projects.filter((item) => item.id !== id));
  };

  const handleAddTech = (projId: string) => {
    const val = (techInputs[projId] || '').trim();
    if (!val) return;

    const proj = projects.find((p) => p.id === projId);
    if (!proj) return;

    const newStack = Array.from(new Set([...proj.techStack, val]));
    handleUpdateProject(projId, { techStack: newStack });
    setTechInputs((prev) => ({ ...prev, [projId]: '' }));
  };

  const handleRemoveTech = (projId: string, tech: string) => {
    const proj = projects.find((p) => p.id === projId);
    if (!proj) return;

    handleUpdateProject(projId, {
      techStack: proj.techStack.filter((t) => t !== tech),
    });
  };

  const handleUpdateBullet = (projId: string, bIdx: number, text: string) => {
    const proj = projects.find((p) => p.id === projId);
    if (!proj) return;

    const newBullets = [...proj.bullets];
    newBullets[bIdx] = text;
    handleUpdateProject(projId, { bullets: newBullets });
  };

  const handleRemoveBullet = (projId: string, bIdx: number) => {
    const proj = projects.find((p) => p.id === projId);
    if (!proj) return;

    const newBullets = proj.bullets.filter((_, idx) => idx !== bIdx);
    const newHidden = (proj.hiddenBullets || [])
      .filter((idx) => idx !== bIdx)
      .map((idx) => (idx > bIdx ? idx - 1 : idx));
    handleUpdateProject(projId, {
      bullets: newBullets,
      hiddenBullets: newHidden,
    });
  };

  const handleAddBullet = (projId: string) => {
    const text = (newBulletInputs[projId] || '').trim();
    if (!text) return;

    const proj = projects.find((p) => p.id === projId);
    if (!proj) return;

    handleUpdateProject(projId, { bullets: [...proj.bullets, text] });
    setNewBulletInputs((prev) => ({ ...prev, [projId]: '' }));
  };

  const handleAiOptimizeBullet = async (projId: string, bIdx: number) => {
    const proj = projects.find((p) => p.id === projId);
    if (!proj) return;

    const bullet = proj.bullets[bIdx];
    if (!bullet) return;

    const optKey = `${projId}_${bIdx}`;
    setOptimizingKey(optKey);
    try {
      const optimized = await onOptimizeBullet(bullet, `Project: ${proj.title}`);
      handleUpdateBullet(projId, bIdx, optimized);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Failed to optimize project bullet:', err);
    } finally {
      setOptimizingKey(null);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="bg-zinc-900/70 border border-zinc-800 rounded-2xl p-6 shadow-xl space-y-6">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between pb-3 border-b border-zinc-800">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="p-2 rounded-xl bg-cyan-500/10 text-cyan-400 border border-cyan-500/20">
            <FolderGit2 className="h-5 w-5" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <h3 className="font-semibold text-white text-base">Key Showcase Projects</h3>
            <p className="text-xs text-zinc-400">
              Aggregated from GitHub repositories and resume projects with quantifiable metrics
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={handleAddProject}
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold bg-cyan-600 hover:bg-cyan-500 text-white transition shadow-sm"
        >
          <Plus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
          Add Project
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-6">
        {projects.map((proj) => {
          const isCollapsed = Boolean(collapsedProjects[proj.id]);
          const isBulletsCollapsed = Boolean(collapsedBullets[proj.id]);
          const hiddenBulletsSet = new Set(proj.hiddenBullets || []);
          const visibleBulletsCount = proj.bullets.length - hiddenBulletsSet.size;

          return (
            <div
              key={proj.id}
              className={`p-5 rounded-2xl border transition space-y-4 ${
                proj.isHidden
                  ? 'bg-zinc-950/40 border-dashed border-amber-500/30 opacity-75'
                  : 'bg-zinc-950/70 border-zinc-800 hover:border-zinc-700/80'
              }`}
            >
              {/* Top row: Title, Role, Collapse toggle & Actions */}
              <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3 pb-3 border-b border-zinc-800/80">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-1">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => toggleCollapseProject(proj.id)}
                    className="p-1 rounded-lg text-zinc-400 hover:text-white hover:bg-zinc-800 transition shrink-0"
                    title={isCollapsed ? 'Expand project details' : 'Collapse project details'}
                  >
                    {isCollapsed ? <ChevronDown className="h-4 w-4" /> : <ChevronUp className="h-4 w-4" />}
                  </button>

                  {isCollapsed ? (
                    <div
                      className="flex-1 cursor-pointer flex items-center justify-between gap-3 flex-wrap"
                      onClick={() => toggleCollapseProject(proj.id)}
                    >
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
                        <span className="font-semibold text-sm text-white">
                          {proj.title || 'Untitled Project'}
                        </span>
                        {proj.role && (
                          <span className="text-xs text-zinc-400">
                            ({proj.role})
                          </span>
                        )}
                        {proj.techStack.length > 0 && (
                          <span className="text-[11px] text-cyan-400/80 font-mono">
                            • {proj.techStack.slice(0, 3).join(', ')}{proj.techStack.length > 3 ? '...' : ''}
                          </span>
                        )}
                      </div>
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        <span className="text-[10px] px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-zinc-800 text-zinc-300 font-mono">
                          {proj.bullets.length} bullets ({visibleBulletsCount} on CV)
                        </span>
                        {proj.isHidden && (
                          <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-semibold bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-400 border border-amber-500/20 flex items-center gap-1">
                            <EyeOff className="h-3 w-3" /> Hidden from CV
                          </span>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  ) : (
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3 flex-1">
                      <div>
                        <label className="block text-[11px] font-medium text-zinc-400 mb-1">
                          Project Title
                        </label>
                        <input
                          type="text"
                          value={proj.title}
                          onChange={(e) =>
                            handleUpdateProject(proj.id, { title: e.target.value })
                          }
                          className="w-full px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800 rounded-lg text-white focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-cyan-500"
                        />
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <label className="block text-[11px] font-medium text-zinc-400 mb-1">
                          Role in Project
                        </label>
                        <input
                          type="text"
                          value={proj.role || ''}
                          onChange={(e) =>
                            handleUpdateProject(proj.id, { role: e.target.value })
                          }
                          placeholder="e.g. Lead Architect / Full-Stack Developer"
                          className="w-full px-3 py-1.5 text-xs bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800 rounded-lg text-white focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-cyan-500"
                        />
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>

                {/* Right Action buttons */}
                <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 self-end sm:self-center shrink-0">
                  {proj.isHidden && !isCollapsed && (
                    <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-semibold bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-400 border border-amber-500/20 flex items-center gap-1">
                      <EyeOff className="h-3 w-3" /> Hidden from CV
                    </span>
                  )}
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => handleToggleHideProject(proj.id)}
                    className={`p-1.5 rounded-lg border transition ${
                      proj.isHidden
                        ? 'bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-400 border-amber-500/30 hover:bg-amber-500/20'
                        : 'bg-zinc-900 text-zinc-400 border-zinc-800 hover:text-white hover:border-zinc-700'
                    }`}
                    title={proj.isHidden ? 'Click to include this project in CV' : 'Click to hide this project from CV'}
                  >
                    {proj.isHidden ? <EyeOff className="h-4 w-4" /> : <Eye className="h-4 w-4" />}
                  </button>

                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => handleRemoveProject(proj.id)}
                    className="p-1.5 rounded-lg text-zinc-500 hover:text-rose-400 hover:bg-zinc-900 transition"
                    title="Remove project"
                  >
                    <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4" />
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Collapsed body is hidden, expanded body shows details */}
              {!isCollapsed && (
                <>
                  {/* Middle row: Impact metric & URLs */}
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3">
                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-[11px] font-medium text-zinc-400 mb-1 flex items-center gap-1">
                        <TrendingUp className="h-3 w-3 text-cyan-400" />
                        Primary Impact Metric
                      </label>
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        value={proj.impactMetric || ''}
                        onChange={(e) =>
                          handleUpdateProject(proj.id, { impactMetric: e.target.value })
                        }
                        placeholder="e.g. Sub-80ms search latency across 10k items"
                        className="w-full px-3 py-1.5 text-xs bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800 rounded-lg text-cyan-300 placeholder-zinc-500 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-cyan-500"
                      />
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-[11px] font-medium text-zinc-400 mb-1 flex items-center gap-1">
                        <GithubIcon className="h-3 w-3 text-zinc-400" />
                        GitHub Repository URL
                      </label>
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        value={proj.githubUrl || ''}
                        onChange={(e) =>
                          handleUpdateProject(proj.id, { githubUrl: e.target.value })
                        }
                        placeholder="https://github.com/..."
                        className="w-full px-3 py-1.5 text-xs bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800 rounded-lg text-zinc-200 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-cyan-500"
                      />
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-[11px] font-medium text-zinc-400 mb-1 flex items-center gap-1">
                        <ExternalLink className="h-3 w-3 text-zinc-400" />
                        Live Demo URL
                      </label>
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        value={proj.demoUrl || ''}
                        onChange={(e) =>
                          handleUpdateProject(proj.id, { demoUrl: e.target.value })
                        }
                        placeholder="https://..."
                        className="w-full px-3 py-1.5 text-xs bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800 rounded-lg text-zinc-200 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-cyan-500"
                      />
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* Tech Stack Chips */}
                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-[11px] font-medium text-zinc-400 mb-1.5">
                      Tech Stack
                    </label>
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5 mb-2">
                      {proj.techStack.map((tech) => (
                        <span
                          key={tech}
                          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md text-[11px] bg-cyan-500/10 text-cyan-300 border border-cyan-500/20"
                        >
                          <span>{tech}</span>
                          <button
                            type="button"
                            onClick={() => handleRemoveTech(proj.id, tech)}
                            className="hover:text-rose-400"
                          >
                            <X className="h-3 w-3" />
                          </button>
                        </span>
                      ))}
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex gap-2 max-w-xs">
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        value={techInputs[proj.id] || ''}
                        onChange={(e) =>
                          setTechInputs((prev) => ({ ...prev, [proj.id]: e.target.value }))
                        }
                        onKeyDown={(e) => {
                          if (e.key === 'Enter') {
                            e.preventDefault();
                            handleAddTech(proj.id);
                          }
                        }}
                        placeholder="Add technology (e.g. Redis)..."
                        className="flex-1 px-2.5 py-1 text-xs bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800 rounded-lg text-white focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-cyan-500"
                      />
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => handleAddTech(proj.id)}
                        className="px-2.5 py-1 text-xs rounded-lg bg-zinc-800 text-zinc-300 hover:bg-zinc-700"
                      >
                        Add
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* Bullets List */}
                  <div className="space-y-3 pt-2">
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between flex-wrap gap-2">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        <label className="block text-[11px] font-semibold text-zinc-300 uppercase tracking-wider">
                          XYZ Project Achievement Bullets ({proj.bullets.length})
                        </label>
                        <span className="text-[10px] text-zinc-400 font-mono">
                          • {visibleBulletsCount} visible on CV
                          {hiddenBulletsSet.size > 0 && (
                            <span className="text-amber-400 ml-1">({hiddenBulletsSet.size} hidden)</span>
                          )}
                        </span>
                      </div>

                      <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                        {proj.bullets.length > 0 && (
                          <>
                            <button
                              type="button"
                              onClick={() => handleToggleHideAllBullets(proj.id)}
                              className="text-[10px] px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-zinc-900 hover:bg-zinc-800 text-zinc-300 border border-zinc-800 transition"
                            >
                              {hiddenBulletsSet.size === proj.bullets.length ? 'Unhide All' : 'Hide All Bullets'}
                            </button>
                            <button
                              type="button"
                              onClick={() => toggleCollapseBullets(proj.id)}
                              className="text-[10px] px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-zinc-900 hover:bg-zinc-800 text-zinc-300 border border-zinc-800 transition flex items-center gap-1"
                            >
                              {isBulletsCollapsed ? <ChevronDown className="h-3 w-3" /> : <ChevronUp className="h-3 w-3" />}
                              {isBulletsCollapsed ? 'Expand Bullets' : 'Collapse Bullets'}
                            </button>
                          </>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    {!isBulletsCollapsed && (
                      <div className="space-y-2.5">
                        {proj.bullets.map((bullet, bIdx) => {
                          const isOptimizing = optimizingKey === `${proj.id}_${bIdx}`;
                          const isBulletHidden = hiddenBulletsSet.has(bIdx);

                          return (
                            <div
                              key={bIdx}
                              className={`p-3 rounded-xl border transition space-y-2 ${
                                isBulletHidden
                                  ? 'bg-zinc-950/40 border-dashed border-amber-500/20 opacity-75'
                                  : 'bg-zinc-900/80 border-zinc-800/80 hover:border-zinc-700/80'
                              }`}
                            >
                              <div className="flex items-start gap-2">
                                <span className={`text-sm mt-1 ${isBulletHidden ? 'text-amber-500/60' : 'text-zinc-500'}`}>
                                  {isBulletHidden ? <EyeOff className="h-3.5 w-3.5 mt-0.5" /> : '•'}
                                </span>
                                <textarea
                                  rows={2}
                                  value={bullet}
                                  onChange={(e) =>
                                    handleUpdateBullet(proj.id, bIdx, e.target.value)
                                  }
                                  className={`flex-1 p-2 text-xs rounded-lg placeholder-zinc-500 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-cyan-500 leading-relaxed resize-none border ${
                                    isBulletHidden
                                      ? 'bg-zinc-950/60 border-dashed border-zinc-800 text-zinc-400'
                                      : 'bg-zinc-950 border-zinc-800 text-zinc-100'
                                  }`}
                                />
                                <button
                                  type="button"
                                  onClick={() => handleToggleHideBullet(proj.id, bIdx)}
                                  className={`p-1.5 rounded-lg border transition ${
                                    isBulletHidden
                                      ? 'bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-400 border-amber-500/30 hover:bg-amber-500/20'
                                      : 'text-zinc-400 hover:text-white border-transparent hover:bg-zinc-800'
                                  }`}
                                  title={isBulletHidden ? 'Include this bullet on CV' : 'Hide this bullet from CV'}
                                >
                                  {isBulletHidden ? <EyeOff className="h-3.5 w-3.5" /> : <Eye className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />}
                                </button>
                                <button
                                  type="button"
                                  onClick={() => handleRemoveBullet(proj.id, bIdx)}
                                  className="p-1.5 rounded-lg text-zinc-500 hover:text-rose-400 hover:bg-zinc-800 transition"
                                  title="Remove bullet"
                                >
                                  <Trash2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                                </button>
                              </div>

                              <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[11px] pt-1">
                                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                                  <span className="text-zinc-500 text-[10px]">
                                    Action Verb ➔ Quantifiable Impact ➔ Architecture Method
                                  </span>
                                  {isBulletHidden && (
                                    <span className="px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded text-[9px] font-semibold bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-400 border border-amber-500/20">
                                      Hidden from CV & PDF
                                    </span>
                                  )}
                                </div>

                                <button
                                  type="button"
                                  disabled={isOptimizing}
                                  onClick={() => handleAiOptimizeBullet(proj.id, bIdx)}
                                  className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-[11px] font-medium bg-cyan-500/10 hover:bg-cyan-500/20 text-cyan-300 border border-cyan-500/30 transition disabled:opacity-50"
                                >
                                  <Sparkles className="h-3 w-3 text-amber-400" />
                                  {isOptimizing ? 'Optimizing...' : '⚡ AI Optimize (XYZ)'}
                                </button>
                              </div>
                            </div>
                          );
                        })}
                      </div>
                    )}

                    {/* Add Bullet Input */}
                    <div className="flex gap-2 pt-1">
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        value={newBulletInputs[proj.id] || ''}
                        onChange={(e) =>
                          setNewBulletInputs((prev) => ({
                            ...prev,
                            [proj.id]: e.target.value,
                          }))
                        }
                        onKeyDown={(e) => {
                          if (e.key === 'Enter') {
                            e.preventDefault();
                            handleAddBullet(proj.id);
                          }
                        }}
                        placeholder="Add a new project accomplishment bullet..."
                        className="flex-1 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800 rounded-lg text-white placeholder-zinc-500 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-cyan-500"
                      />
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => handleAddBullet(proj.id)}
                        className="px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium rounded-lg bg-zinc-800 hover:bg-zinc-700 text-zinc-200 border border-zinc-700 transition flex items-center gap-1"
                      >
                        <Plus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                        Add Bullet
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </>
              )}
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};
</file>

<file path="src/components/studio/SkillsEditor.tsx">
'use client';

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Cpu, Plus, X, Layers } from 'lucide-react';
import { CategorizedSkills } from '@/types/masterCv';

interface SkillsEditorProps {
  skills: CategorizedSkills;
  onChange: (updated: CategorizedSkills) => void;
}

const CATEGORIES: {
  key: keyof CategorizedSkills;
  label: string;
  placeholder: string;
  color: string;
}[] = [
  {
    key: 'languages',
    label: 'Languages',
    placeholder: 'e.g. TypeScript, PHP, Python, SQL',
    color: 'border-blue-500/30 text-blue-300 bg-blue-500/10',
  },
  {
    key: 'frameworks',
    label: 'Frameworks & Libraries',
    placeholder: 'e.g. Laravel, Vue.js, Next.js, Node.js',
    color: 'border-purple-500/30 text-purple-300 bg-purple-500/10',
  },
  {
    key: 'databases',
    label: 'Databases & Optimization',
    placeholder: 'e.g. PostgreSQL, MySQL, Redis, Query Profiling',
    color: 'border-emerald-500/30 text-emerald-300 bg-emerald-500/10',
  },
  {
    key: 'devopsAndCloud',
    label: 'DevOps & Cloud Infrastructure',
    placeholder: 'e.g. Docker, CI/CD, Linux, Nginx, Caddy, AWS',
    color: 'border-amber-500/30 text-amber-300 bg-amber-500/10',
  },
  {
    key: 'toolsAndConcepts',
    label: 'Security & Architecture',
    placeholder: 'e.g. OAuth, JWT, SSL/TLS, AWS IAM, RESTful APIs, Webhooks',
    color: 'border-cyan-500/30 text-cyan-300 bg-cyan-500/10',
  },
];

export const SkillsEditor: React.FC<SkillsEditorProps> = ({ skills, onChange }) => {
  const [inputs, setInputs] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});

  const handleAddSkill = (catKey: keyof CategorizedSkills) => {
    const val = (inputs[catKey] || '').trim();
    if (!val) return;

    // Check for comma-separated items
    const newItems = val
      .split(',')
      .map((s) => s.trim())
      .filter((s) => s.length > 0 && !skills[catKey].includes(s));

    if (newItems.length > 0) {
      onChange({
        ...skills,
        [catKey]: [...skills[catKey], ...newItems],
      });
    }

    setInputs((prev) => ({ ...prev, [catKey]: '' }));
  };

  const handleRemoveSkill = (catKey: keyof CategorizedSkills, skillToRemove: string) => {
    onChange({
      ...skills,
      [catKey]: skills[catKey].filter((s) => s !== skillToRemove),
    });
  };

  const totalSkillsCount = Object.values(skills).reduce((acc, curr) => acc + curr.length, 0);

  return (
    <div className="bg-zinc-900/70 border border-zinc-800 rounded-2xl p-6 shadow-xl space-y-6">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between pb-3 border-b border-zinc-800">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="p-2 rounded-xl bg-purple-500/10 text-purple-400 border border-purple-500/20">
            <Cpu className="h-5 w-5" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <h3 className="font-semibold text-white text-base">Categorized Technical Skills</h3>
            <p className="text-xs text-zinc-400">
              Formatted according to standard ATS resume categories
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <span className="text-xs px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full bg-zinc-800 text-zinc-300 border border-zinc-700">
          {totalSkillsCount} Skills Listed
        </span>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-5">
        {CATEGORIES.map(({ key, label, placeholder, color }) => {
          const list = skills[key] || [];
          const currentInput = inputs[key] || '';

          return (
            <div key={key} className="p-4 rounded-xl bg-zinc-950/60 border border-zinc-800/80">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-2">
                <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-200 uppercase tracking-wider">
                  {label} ({list.length})
                </label>
              </div>

              {/* Tag Cloud */}
              <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5 mb-3 min-h-[30px]">
                {list.map((skill) => (
                  <span
                    key={skill}
                    className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-medium border ${color} transition`}
                  >
                    <span>{skill}</span>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => handleRemoveSkill(key, skill)}
                      className="hover:opacity-75 focus:outline-none"
                    >
                      <X className="h-3 w-3" />
                    </button>
                  </span>
                ))}
                {list.length === 0 && (
                  <span className="text-xs text-zinc-600 italic">No skills added yet</span>
                )}
              </div>

              {/* Add Tag Input */}
              <div className="flex gap-2">
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={currentInput}
                  onChange={(e) =>
                    setInputs((prev) => ({ ...prev, [key]: e.target.value }))
                  }
                  onKeyDown={(e) => {
                    if (e.key === 'Enter') {
                      e.preventDefault();
                      handleAddSkill(key);
                    }
                  }}
                  placeholder={placeholder}
                  className="flex-1 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800 rounded-lg text-white placeholder-zinc-500 focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-purple-500"
                />
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => handleAddSkill(key)}
                  className="px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-medium rounded-lg bg-zinc-800 hover:bg-zinc-700 text-zinc-200 border border-zinc-700 transition flex items-center gap-1"
                >
                  <Plus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                  Add
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};
</file>

<file path="src/components/tailor/ContextualTailorView.tsx">
'use client';

import React, { useState, useEffect, useMemo } from 'react';
import { MasterCvProfile } from '@/types/masterCv';
import { JobDescriptionData, TailoredCvProfile } from '@/types/tailoring';
import { SAMPLE_JOB_POSTINGS, analyzeJdMatch } from '@/lib/tailoringEngine/jdAnalyzer';
import { JobDescriptionInput } from './JobDescriptionInput';
import { MatchScoreCard } from './MatchScoreCard';
import { SideBySideDiffViewer } from './SideBySideDiffViewer';
import {
  Target,
  Download,
  Printer,
  Sparkles,
  BookmarkCheck,
  CheckCircle2,
  FileCheck,
  Zap,
  ArrowRight,
  Loader2,
} from 'lucide-react';

interface ContextualTailorViewProps {
  masterProfile: MasterCvProfile;
  apiKey?: string;
  onNavigateToStage5?: () => void;
}

export const ContextualTailorView: React.FC<ContextualTailorViewProps> = ({
  masterProfile,
  apiKey,
  onNavigateToStage5,
}) => {
  const [currentJd, setCurrentJd] = useState<JobDescriptionData>(SAMPLE_JOB_POSTINGS[0]);
  const [tailoredCv, setTailoredCv] = useState<TailoredCvProfile | null>(null);
  const [isTailoring, setIsTailoring] = useState(false);
  const [isDownloadingPdf, setIsDownloadingPdf] = useState(false);
  const [savedSuccess, setSavedSuccess] = useState(false);

  // Compute baseline analysis whenever currentJd or masterProfile changes
  const baselineAnalysis = useMemo(() => {
    return analyzeJdMatch(masterProfile, currentJd);
  }, [masterProfile, currentJd]);

  // When JD changes, reset active tailoredCv unless already tailored for this JD
  useEffect(() => {
    if (tailoredCv && tailoredCv.jobId !== currentJd.id) {
      setTailoredCv(null);
    }
  }, [currentJd, tailoredCv]);

  // Trigger AI Contextual Tailoring
  const handleGenerateTailored = async () => {
    setIsTailoring(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/tailor/generate', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          masterProfile,
          jobDescription: currentJd,
          apiKey,
        }),
      });

      const data = await res.json();
      if (!res.ok || !data.success) {
        throw new Error(data.error || 'Failed to tailor CV');
      }

      setTailoredCv(data.tailoredCv);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Error generating tailored CV:', err);
      alert('Could not tailor CV: ' + err.message);
    } finally {
      setIsTailoring(false);
    }
  };

  // Download Tailored Vector PDF
  const handleDownloadTailoredPdf = async () => {
    const profileToRender = tailoredCv ? tailoredCv.profile : masterProfile;
    try {
      setIsDownloadingPdf(true);
      const res = await fetch('/api/cv/download-pdf', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ profile: profileToRender }),
      });

      if (!res.ok) {
        throw new Error('Failed to generate tailored ATS PDF');
      }

      const blob = await res.blob();
      const url = window.URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      const a = document.createElement('a');
      a.href = url;
      const safeName = (profileToRender.personalInfo.fullName || 'Candidate').replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g, '_');
      const safeCompany = (currentJd.company || 'Job').replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g, '_');
      a.download = `${safeName}_${safeCompany}_Tailored_Resume.pdf`;
      document.body.appendChild(a);
      a.click();
      window.URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
      document.body.removeChild(a);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Error downloading tailored PDF:', err);
      alert('Could not download PDF: ' + err.message);
    } finally {
      setIsDownloadingPdf(false);
    }
  };

  // Save to application pipeline (stored in localStorage for Stage 5)
  // Save to application pipeline (stored in localStorage for Stage 5)
  const handleSaveToPipeline = () => {
    try {
      const existingRaw = localStorage.getItem('huntflow_applications');
      const existing = existingRaw ? JSON.parse(existingRaw) : [];
      const now = new Date().toISOString();
      const score = tailoredCv ? tailoredCv.analysis.matchPercentage : baselineAnalysis.matchPercentage;
      const newApp = {
        id: `app_${Date.now()}`,
        jobTitle: currentJd.jobTitle,
        company: currentJd.company,
        location: currentJd.location,
        workType: currentJd.workType,
        matchScore: score,
        status: 'wishlist',
        timeline: [
          {
            id: `evt_${Date.now()}`,
            status: 'wishlist',
            date: now,
            note: `Created from Job Tailoring Studio with ${score}% match score`,
          },
        ],
        tailoredCvSnapshot: tailoredCv ? tailoredCv.profile : masterProfile,
        createdAt: now,
        updatedAt: now,
      };
      existing.unshift(newApp);
      localStorage.setItem('huntflow_applications', JSON.stringify(existing));
      setSavedSuccess(true);
      setTimeout(() => setSavedSuccess(false), 4000);
    } catch (e) {
      console.error('Error saving application:', e);
    }
  };

  const activeAnalysis = tailoredCv ? tailoredCv.analysis : baselineAnalysis;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* Top Banner Bar */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4 p-5 rounded-2xl bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-800 shadow-lg">
        <div>
          <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-zinc-100 flex items-center gap-2.5">
            <Target className="h-6 w-6 text-purple-400" />
            Contextual Job Tailoring Engine
          </h2>
          <p className="text-xs text-zinc-400 mt-1">
            Real-time JD keyword gap analyzer, dynamic skill re-ranker, and targeted ATS resume generator.
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Global Action Buttons */}
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleDownloadTailoredPdf}
            disabled={isDownloadingPdf}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold bg-gradient-to-r from-purple-600 via-indigo-600 to-emerald-600 hover:from-purple-500 hover:to-emerald-500 text-white shadow-lg shadow-indigo-950/40 transition disabled:opacity-50"
          >
            {isDownloadingPdf ? (
              <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" />
            ) : (
              <Download className="h-4 w-4" />
            )}
            Download Tailored Vector PDF
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleSaveToPipeline}
            className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold border transition ${
              savedSuccess
                ? 'bg-emerald-950/40 border-emerald-500/50 text-emerald-300'
                : 'bg-zinc-800 hover:bg-zinc-700 text-zinc-200 border-zinc-700'
            }`}
          >
            <BookmarkCheck className={`h-3.5 w-3.5 ${savedSuccess ? 'text-emerald-400' : 'text-zinc-400'}`} />
            {savedSuccess ? 'Saved to Pipeline!' : 'Save Application'}
          </button>

          {onNavigateToStage5 && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onNavigateToStage5}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold bg-indigo-600/20 hover:bg-indigo-600/30 text-indigo-300 border border-indigo-500/30 transition"
            >
              View Pipeline
              <ArrowRight className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* STEP 1: Job Description Input */}
      <JobDescriptionInput
        currentJd={currentJd}
        onSelectJd={(jd) => setCurrentJd(jd)}
        isLoading={isTailoring}
      />

      {/* STEP 2: Match Score & Gap Analysis */}
      <MatchScoreCard
        analysis={activeAnalysis}
        diffSummary={tailoredCv?.diffSummary}
        isTailored={Boolean(tailoredCv)}
        onGenerateTailored={handleGenerateTailored}
        isTailoring={isTailoring}
      />

      {/* STEP 3: Side-by-Side Diff Inspector & Tailored Document */}
      {tailoredCv ? (
        <SideBySideDiffViewer
          masterProfile={masterProfile}
          tailoredProfile={tailoredCv}
        />
      ) : (
        <div className="p-8 rounded-2xl bg-zinc-950 border border-dashed border-zinc-800 text-center space-y-3">
          <Sparkles className="h-8 w-8 text-indigo-400 mx-auto" />
          <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-zinc-200">
            Ready to Tailor Your Master CV for {currentJd.company}
          </h4>
          <p className="text-xs text-zinc-400 max-w-md mx-auto">
            Click &quot;⚡ Generate Contextually Tailored CV&quot; above to re-rank your skills, contextualize your summary, and highlight relevant achievements for this role.
          </p>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleGenerateTailored}
            disabled={isTailoring}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-500 text-white transition shadow-sm"
          >
            {isTailoring ? (
              <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" />
            ) : (
              <Zap className="h-4 w-4 text-amber-300" />
            )}
            Tailor CV Now
          </button>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};
</file>
